  Paracittavijānanāya pana atīte sattadivasesu anāgate sattadivasesu ca pavattaṃ
  parittādīsu yaṅkiñci tikālikaṃ cittameva ārammaṇaṃ hotīti
  mahāaṭṭhakathācariyā. Saṅgahakārā pana cattāro khandhātipi vadanti.
  Kathaṃ panassa 1- paccuppannacittālambanatā nanu āvajjanāya gahitameva
  sattadivase 2- iddhicittassa ārammaṇaṃ hoti āvajjanāya [3]-
  paccuppannacittālambanaṃ katvā nirujjhamānāya taṃsamakālameva parassa cittampi
  nirujjhatīti āvajjanajavanānaṃ kālavasena ekārammaṇatā na siyā
  maggaphalavīthito aññattha ca javanāvajjanānaṃ kathañci 4- nānārammaṇatā
  na adhippetāti. Aṭṭhakathāyantāva santatiaddhānapaccuppannālambanatā
  yojitā. Ānandācariyo pana bhaṇati pādakajjhānato vuṭṭhāya
  paccuppannādivibhāgaṃ akatvā kevalaṃ imassa cittaṃ jānāmicceva
  parikammaṃ katvā punapi pādakajjhānaṃ samāpajjitvā vuṭṭhāya aviseseneva
  cittaṃ āvajjetvā tiṇṇaṃ catunnaṃ vā parikammānamanantaraṃ
  cetopariyañāṇena parassa cittaṃ paṭivijjhati rūpaṃ viya dibbacakkhunā
  pacchā kāmāvacaracittena sarāgādivavatthānaṃ karoti nīlādivavatthānaṃ
  viya tāni ca sabbāni abhimukhībhūtacittālambanāneva aniṭṭheva
  ṭhāne ca nānālambanatādoso natthi abhinnākārappavattitoti.
  Pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇassa pubbenivuṭṭhakkhandhā khandhappaṭibaddhāni ca
  nāmagottāni ca nibbānañca ārammaṇaṃ hoti dibbacakkhussa pana
  rūpameva paccuppannanti. Ayametesaṃ ārammaṇavibhāgo.
  Yathārahamappetīti taṃtaṃparikammānurūpato appeti.

      Idāni ārammaṇānaṃ bhedeneva abhiññānaṃ bhedaṃ dassetuṃ
  iddhividhātiādimāha. Adhiṭṭhānādi iddhippabhedo etissāti iddhividhā.
  Dibbānaṃ sotasadisatāya dibbavihārasannissayatāya 1- ca dibbañca
  taṃ sotañcāti dibbasotaṃ. Paresaṃ cittaṃ viññāyati etāyāti
  paracittavijānanā. Attano santāne vuṭṭhavasena ceva gocaranivāsanavasena
  ca pubbe atītabhavesu khandhādīnaṃ anussaraṇaṃ pubbenivāsānussati.
  Vuttanayena dibbañca taṃ cakkhuñcāti dibbacakkhu. Cutūpapātañāṇanti
  pana dibbacakkhumeva vuccati. Yathākammopagañāṇānāgataṃsañāṇānipi
  dibbacakkhuvaseneva ijjhanti. Na hi tesaṃ visuṃ
  parikammaṃ atthi. Tattha anāgataṃsañāṇassa tāva anāgate sattadivasato
  paraṃ pavatatanakacittacetasikaṃ dutiyadivasato paṭṭhāya pavattanakañca
  yaṅkiñci ārammaṇaṃ hoti. Tañhi savisaye sabbaññutañāṇagatikanti
  vuttaṃ. Yathākammopagañāṇassa pana kusalākusalasaṅkhātā
  cetanā cattāropi vā khandhā ārammaṇanti daṭṭhabbaṃ. Gocaravasena
  bhedo gocarabhedo.
                     Samathakammaṭṭhānanayo.
      Aniccādivasena vividhākārena passatīti vipassanā

  kammaṭṭhānanti vipassanākammaṭṭhānaṃ. Tasmiṃ. Sattavidhena
  visuddhisaṅgahoti sambandho.
      Aniccatāyeva lakkhaṇaṃ lakkhitabbaṃ lakkhiyati anenāti vā

  Aniccalakkhaṇaṃ. Udayavayapaṭipīḷanasaṅkhātadukkhabhāvoyeva lakkhaṇanti
  dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ. Paraparikappitassa attano ca abhāvo anattatā.
  Tadeva lakkhaṇanti anattalakkhaṇaṃ. Tiṇṇaṃ lakkhaṇānaṃ anupassanā
  aniccānupassanādikā.
      Khandhādīnaṃ kalāpato sammasanavasappavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ sammasanañāṇaṃ.
  Uppādabhaṅgānupassanāvasappavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ udayabbayañāṇaṃ. Udayaṃ
  muñcitvā vaye pavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ bhaṅgañāṇaṃ. Saṅkhārānaṃ bhayato
  anupassanāvasena pavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ bhayañāṇaṃ. Diṭṭhabhayānaṃ ādīnavato
  pekkhanavasena pavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ ādīnavañāṇaṃ. Diṭṭhādīnavesu
  nibbindanavasappavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ nibbedañāṇaṃ 1-. Nibbinditvā saṅkhārehi
  muñcitukamyatāvasena pavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ muñcitukamyatāñāṇaṃ. Muñcanassa
  upāyasampaṭipādanatthaṃ puna saṅkhārānaṃ pariggahavasappavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ
  paṭisaṅkhāñāṇaṃ. Paṭisaṅkhātadhammesu bhayanandivivajjanavasena
  ajjhupekkhitvā pavattaṃ ñāṇaṃ saṅkhārupekkhāñāṇaṃ. Purimānaṃ navannaṃ
  kiccanipphattiyā upari ca sattattiṃsāya bodhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ anukulaṃ
  ñāṇaṃ anulomañāṇaṃ.

  vimokkho. Niccanimittādino abhāvato animitto. Paṇihitassa
  taṇhāpaṇidhissa abhāvato appaṇihito.
      Yo naṃ pāti taṃ pātiṃ mokkheti apāyādīhi dukkhehīti pāṭimokkhaṃ.

  Sīlañcāti pāṭimokkhasaṃvarasīlaṃ. Manacchaṭṭhānaṃ indriyānaṃ rūpādīsu
  saṃvaraṇavasena pavattaṃ sīlaṃ indriyasaṃvarasīlaṃ. Micchājīvavivajjanena
  ājīvassa parisuddhivasappavattaṃ sīlaṃ ājīvapārisuddhisīlaṃ. Paccaye

  Catubbidhattā desanāsaṃvarapariyeṭṭhipaccavekkhaṇavasena parisuddhattā ca
  catupārisuddhisīlaṃ.
      Cittavisuddhi nāma cittassa vinīvaraṇabhāvāpādanavasena visodhanato
  cittasīsena niddiṭṭhattā visuddhattā cāti vā katvā. Dhammānaṃ
  sāmaññasabhāvo lakkhaṇaṃ kiccasampattiyo raso upaṭṭhānākāro
  phalañca paccupaṭṭhānanti evaṃ vuttānaṃ lakkhaṇādīnaṃ phusanalakkhaṇo
  phasso kakkhalalakkhaṇā paṭhavītiādinā vitthārato namanalakkhaṇaṃ
  nāmaṃ ruppanalakkhaṇaṃ rūpantiādinā saṅkhepato ca pariggaho
  paccattalakkhaṇādivasena paricchijja gahaṇaṃ dukkhasaccavavatthānaṃ
  diṭṭhivisuddhi nāma nāmarūpato añño attā nāma natthīti dassanato
  diṭṭhi ca attadiṭṭhimalavisodhanato visuddhi cāti katvā.
      Paccayapariggahoti nāmarūpañca 1- paṭisandhiyaṃ tāva
  avijjātaṇhāupādānakammahetuvasena nibbattati pavattiyañca rūpaṃ
  kammacittautuāhārapaccayavasena nāmañca cakkhurūpādinissayārammaṇādi-
  paccayavasena visesato ca yonisomanasikārādicatukkasampattiyā
  kusalaṃ tabbipariyāyena akusalaṃ kusalākusalavasena vipāko bhavaṅgādivasena
  āvajjanaṃ khīṇāsavasantānavasena kriyājavanañca 2- uppajjatīti

  Evaṃ sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇavasena tīsu addhāsu nāmarūpappavattiyā
  paccakkhādisiddhassa kammādipaccayassa pariggahaṇaṃ samudayasaccavavatthānaṃ
  kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhi nāma ahosiṃ nu kho ahamatītamaddhānantiādikāya
  soḷasavidhāya satthari kaṅkhātiādikāya aṭṭhavidhāya ca kaṅkhāya
  vitaraṇato atikkamanato kaṅkhāvitaraṇā ahetuvisamahetudiṭṭhimalavisodhanato 1-
  visuddhi cāti katvā.
      Tato paccayapariggahato paraṃ tathā pariggahitesu
  paccattalakkhaṇādivavatthānavasena paccayavavatthānavasena ca pariggahitesu
  lokuttaravajjesu tibhūmipariyāpannesu nāmarūpesu atītādibhedabhinnesu
  khandhādinayamārabbha pañcakkhandhachadvārachaḷārammaṇachadvārappavattadhammādivasena
  āgataṃ khandhādinayamārabbha kalāpavasena piṇḍavasena saṅkhipitvā
  yaṃ atīte jātaṃ rūpaṃ taṃ atīteyeva niruddhaṃ yaṃ anāgate bhavissati 2-
  rūpaṃ tampi tattheva nirujjhissati yaṃ paccuppannaṃ taṃ anāgataṃ appatvā
  ettheva nirujjhissati 3- tathā ajjhattabahiddhāsukhumaoḷārikahīnapaṇītarūpādayo
  tasmā aniccaṃ attādivasena na icchitabbaṃ 4- anupagantabbaṃ
  khayatthena khayagamanato dukkhaṃ bhayatthena bhayakaraṇato anattā
  asārakatthena attasārādiabhāvenāti ca cakkhuṃ aniccaṃ .pe.
  Mano rūpaṃ .pe. Dhammā cakkhuviññāṇaṃ .pe. Manoviññāṇaṃ
  aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ anattāti ādinā atītādiaddhānavasena atītādisantānavasena
  atītādikkhaṇavasena ca sammasanañāṇena hutvāabhāvaudayabbaya-
  paṭipīḷanāvasavattanākārasaṅkhātalakkhaṇattayasammasanavasappavattena

  4. Sī. Ma. na iccaṃ. chaṭṭhaparicchedavaṇṇanāyampi niccadhuvasabhāvena na
  iccaṃ...aniccanti vuttaṃ.

[page 271]
  Kalāpasammasanañāṇena lakkhaṇattayaṃ sammasantassa parimajjantassa
  sammasanañāṇe pana uppanne puna tesveva saṅkhāresu
  avijjāsamudayā rūpasamudayo taṇhākammāahārasamudayā rūpasamudayo tathā
  avijjānirodhā rūpanirodho taṇhākammāahāranirodhā rūpanirodhoti
  evaṃ rūpakkhandhe vedanāsaññāsaṅkhārakkhandhesupi āhāraṃ apanetvā
  phassasamudayā phassanirodhāti ca evaṃ phassaṃ pakkhipitvā viññāṇakkhandhe
  nāmarūpasamudayā nāmarūpanirodhāti nāmarūpaṃ pakkhipitvā paccayasamudayavasena
  paccayanirodhavasena ca paccaye anāmasitvā paccuppannakkhaṇesu
  nibbattilakkhaṇamattassa vipariṇāmalakkhaṇamattassa ca dassanena
  khaṇavasena cāti ekekasmiṃ khandhe paccayavasena catudhā khaṇavasena ekadhā
  cāti pañcadhā udayaṃ pañcadhā vayanti dasadasaudayabbayadassanavasena
  samapaññāsākārehi udayabbayañāṇena udayabbayaṃ samanupassantassa
  āraddhavipassakassa yogino vipassanācittasamuṭṭhāno sarīrato
  niccharaṇālokasaṅkhāto obhāso vipassanācittasahajātā
  khuddakādipañcavidhā pīti tathā kāyacittadarathavūpasamanalakkhaṇā kāyacittavasena
  duvidhā passaddhi balavasaddhindriyasaṅkhāto adhimokkho
  sammappadhānakiccasādhako viriyasambojjhaṅgasaṅkhāto paggaho atipaṇītaṃ sukhaṃ
  indavisaṭṭhavajirasadisaṃ tilakkhaṇavipassanābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ satipaṭṭhānabhūtā
  cirakatādianussaraṇasamatthā upaṭṭhānasaṅkhātā sati sammappavattavipassanāsahajātā
  upekkhāsambojjhaṅgabhūtā tatramajjhattupekkhā
  manodvāre āvajjanupekkhā cāti duvidhāpi upekkhāti 1- obhāsādīsu

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. sabbattha potthakesu yebhuyyena itisaddo natthi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 272]
  Navasu uppannesu na vata me ito pubbe evarūpo obhāso
  uppannapubbotiādinā nayena tattha ālayaṃ kurumānā sukhumataṇhā
  rūpanikanti cāti obhāsādīsu dasasu vipassanūpakilesesu uppannesu
  na vata me evarūpā obhāsādayo uppannapubbā addhā
  maggappattosmi phalappattosmīti aggahetvā ime obhāsādayo
  taṇhāmānavatthutāya na maggo athakho vipassanāupakilesā eva
  tabbinimuttaṃ pana vīthipaṭipannaṃ vipassanāñāṇaṃ maggoti evaṃ maggalakkhaṇassa
  vavatthānaṃ nicchayanaṃ maggāmaggassa jānanato dassanato amagge
  maggasaññāvisodhanato ca maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhi nāma.
      Yāvānulomāti yāva saccānulomañāṇā. Nava
  vipassanāñāṇānīti khandhānaṃ udayañca vayañca jānanakaṃ udayabbayañāṇaṃ udayaṃ
  muñcitvā 1- bhaṅgamattānupekkhaṃ bhaṅgañāṇaṃ bhaṅgavasena upaṭṭhitānaṃ
  sīhādīnaṃ viya bhāyitabbākārānupekkhakaṃ bhayañāṇaṃ tathānupekkhitānaṃ
  ādittagharassa viya ādīnavākārānupekkhakaṃ ādīnavañāṇaṃ
  diṭṭhādīnavesu nibbindanavasena pavattaṃ nibbedañāṇaṃ jālādito macchādiko 2-
  viya tehi tehi tebhūmikadhammehi muñcitukamyatāvasena pavattaṃ
  muñcitukamyatāñāṇaṃ muñcanupāyasampādanatthaṃ diṭṭhādīnavesupi samuddasakuṇī 3-
  viya punappunaṃ sammasanavasappavattaṃ paṭisaṅkhānupassanāñāṇaṃ
  cattabhariyo puriso viya diṭṭhādīnavesu tesu saṅkhāresu upekkhanākārappavattaṃ
  saṅkhārupekkhāñāṇaṃ aniccādilakkhaṇavisayatāya heṭṭhā pavattānaṃ
  aṭṭhannaṃ vipassanāñāṇānaṃ uddhaṃ maggakkhaṇe adhigantabbānaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Ma. muccitvā. 2. Sī. Ma. macchādikā. 3. samuddasakuṇī samudde gacchantī
  kākīti yojanā. aññattha panesā disākākoti vuccati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 273]
  Sattatiṃsabodhipakkhiyadhammānañca anulomato maggavīthiyaṃ gotrabhūto pubbe
  pavattaṃ saccānulomikañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ navamaṃ anulomañāṇanti imāni
  nava ñāṇāni ñāṇadassanavisuddhiyā paṭipadābhāvato tilakkhaṇajānanatthena
  paccakkhato dassanatthena paṭipakkhato visuddhattā ca
  paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi nāma.
                       Chavisuddhinayo.
      Vipassanāya paripāko saṅkhārupekkhāñāṇaṃ taṃ āgamma paṭicca.
  Idāni appanā uppajjissatīti idāni appanāsaṅakhāto lokuttaramaggo
  uppajjissatīti vattabbakkhaṇe. Yaṅkiñcīti saṅkhārupekkhāya
  gahitesu tīsu ekaṃ yaṅkiñci. Vipassanāya matthakappattiyā sikhappattā
  anulomañāṇasahitāya sānulomā. Sā eva saṅkhāresu udāsīnattā
  saṅkhārupekkhā yathānurūpaṃ apāyādito saṅkhāranimittato ca vuṭṭhahanato
  vuṭṭhānasaṅkhātaṃ maggaṃ gacchatīti vuṭṭhānagāminī. Abhisambhontanti
  pāpuṇantaṃ. Parijānantoti ettakaṃ dukkhaṃ na ito ūnādhikanti
  paricchijja jānanto. Sacchikarontoti ārammaṇakaraṇavasena
  paccakkhaṃ karonto. Maggasaccabhāvanāvasenāti maggasaccasaṅkhātassa
  sampayuttamaggaṅgasaṅkhātassa catutthasaccassa sahajātādipaccayo hutvā
  vaḍḍhanavasena. Ekasseva ñāṇassa catukiccasādhanaṃ padīpādīnaṃ
  vaṭṭiḍāhādicatukiccadassanato yo bhikkhave dukkhaṃ passatītiādiāgamato
  ca sampaṭicchitabbaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 274]
      Dve tīṇi phalacittāni pavattitvāti magguppattiyā anurūpato
  dve vā tīṇi vā phalacittāni apanītaggimhi ṭhāne uṇhattanibbāpanatthāya
  ghaṭehi abhisañciyamānamudakaṃ viya samucchinne kilesepi
  santāne darathapaṭippassambhakāni hutvā pavattitvā. Tesaṃ pavattiyāti
  vuttaṃ hoti. Paccavekkhaṇañāṇānīti maggaphalādivisayāni
  kāmāvacarañāṇāni yāni sandhāya vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hotīti vuttaṃ.
      Idāni paccavekkhaṇāya bhūmiṃ dassetuṃ maggaṃ phalañcātiādi
  vuttaṃ. Tattha imināhaṃ 1- maggenāgatoti maggañca paccavekkhati.
  Tato ayaṃ nāma me ānisaṃso laddhoti tassa phalaṃ. Tato
  ayaṃ nāma me dhammo ārammaṇato sacchikatoti nibbānañca
  paṇḍito paccavekkhati. Tato ime nāma me kilesā pahīnāti
  pahīnakilese. Ime nāma avasiṭṭhāti avasiṭṭhakilese. Paccavekkhati
  vā na vāti koci sekkho paccavekkhati koci na paccavekkhati.
  Tattha kāmāvacaroti 2- adhippāyo.
      Tathāhi mahānāmasakyo ko su nāma me bhante dhammo
  ajjhattaṃ appahīnoti 3- appahīne kilese pucchi. Arahato pana
  avasiṭṭhakilesapaccavekkhaṇaṃ natthi sabbakilesānaṃ pahīnattā. Tasmā
  tiṇṇaṃ sekkhānaṃ paṇṇarasa arahato cattārīti ekūnavīsatipaccavekkhaṇañāṇānīti
  daṭṭhabbaṃ. Chabbisuddhikkamenāti sīlacittavisuddhivasena
  mūlabhūtānaṃ dvinnaṃ diṭṭhivisuddhiādīnaṃ vasena sarīrabhūtānaṃ catunnanti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Sī. Ma. iminā vatāhaṃ. 2. Sī. Ma. kāmacāro. 3. Ma. mū. 12/179.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 275]
  Etāsaṃ channaṃ visuddhīnaṃ kamena. Catunnaṃ saccānaṃ jānanato
  paccavekkhaṇakaraṇato 1- kilesamalehi visuddhattā ca ñāṇadassanavisuddhi
  nāma. Etthāti vipassanākammaṭṭhāne.
                     Sattavisuddhiniddeso.
      Tatthāti tasmiṃ uddese. Saṅkhāresu yo attābhiniveso
  kammassa kārako phalassa ca vedako eso me attāti evaṃ
  abhiniveso daḷhaggāho taṃ muñcantī anattāti pavattā anupassanāva
  attasuññatākārānupassanato suññatānupassanā nāma vimokkhamukhaṃ
  paṭipakkhato vimuttivasena vimokkhasaṅkhātassa lokuttaramaggaphalassa dvāraṃ
  hoti. Saṅkhāresu aniccanti pavattā anupassanā anicce niccanti
  pavattaṃ saññācittadiṭṭhivipallāsasaṅkhātaṃ vipallāsanimittaṃ muñcantī
  pajahantī vipallāsanimittarahitākārānupassanato animittānupassanā nāma
  vimokkhamukhaṃ hotīti sambandho. Dukkhanti pavattā anupassanā saṅkhāresu
  etaṃ mama etaṃ sukhantiādinā nayena pavattaṃ kāma bhavataṇhāsaṅkhātaṃ
  taṇhāpaṇidhiṃ taṇhāpatthanaṃ muñcantī dukkhākāradassanena pariccajantī
  paṇidhirahitākārānupassanato appaṇihitānupassanā nāma.
      Tasmāti yasmā etāsaṃ tissannaṃ etāni tīṇi nāmāni
  tasmā yadi vuṭṭhānagāminīvipassanā anattato vipassati. Maggo
  suññato nāma vimokkho hoti āgamanavasena laddhanāmattā.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. katthaci paccakkhakaraṇatoti dissati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 276]
  Vipassanāgamanavasenāti vipassanāsaṅkhātāgamanavasena. Āgacchati etena
  maggo phalañcāti vipassanāmaggo idhāgamanaṃ nāma. Yathāvuttanayenāti
  pubbe vuttānattānupassanādivasena. Yathāsakaṃ phalamuppajjamānānampīti
  yathāladdhamaggassa phalabhūtaṃ attano attano phalaṃ uppajjamānampi
  maggāgamanavasena alabhitvā vipassanāgamanavaseneva tīṇi nāmāni labhati
  tadā maggappavattiabhāvena tassa dvārabhāvāyogato. Ālambanavasenāti
  sabbasaṅkhārasuññattā saṅkhāranimittarahitattā taṇhāpaṇidhirahitattā
  ca suññatānimittappaṇihitanāmavantaṃ nibbānaṃ ārabbha
  pavattattā tassa vasena. Sarasavasenāti rāgādisuññattā
  rūpanimittādiārammaṇarahitattā kilesapaṇidhirahitattā attano guṇavasena.
  Sabbatthāpīti maggavīthiyaṃ phalasamāpattivīthiyañca. Sabbesampīti
  maggassa phalassapi.
      Sattakkhattuṃ satta vāre 1- kāmasugatiyaṃ paṭisandhiggahaṇaṃ paramaṃ
  etassāti sattakkhattuparamo. Na pana aṭṭhamādikāmabhavaṅgāmīti 2-
  adhippāyo. Yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ na te bhavaṃ aṭṭhamamādiyantīti.
  Rūpārūpasugatibhavaṃ pana sattavārato parampi gacchatīti ācariyā.
  Rāgadosamohānanti mohaggahaṇaṃ rāgadosekaṭṭhamohaṃ sandhāyāti daṭṭhabbaṃ.
  Khīṇā cattāro āsavā etassāti khīṇāsavo. Dakkhiṇārahesu
  aggattā aggadakkhiṇeyyo.
      Sabbesampīti catunnampi ariyapuggalānaṃ. Cittacetasikānaṃ
  appavattisaṅkhātassa nirodhassa samāpatti nirodhasamāpatti diṭṭheva

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Sī. Ma. sattasu vāresu. 2. Ma. aṭṭhamakārāmiti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 277]
  Dhamme cittanirodhaṃ patvā viharaṇaṃ. Anāgāmīnañcāti
  kāmarūpabhavaṭṭhānaṃ aṭṭhasamāpattilābhīnameva anāgāmīnaṃ. Tathā
  khīṇāsavānañca. Tatthāti nirodhasamāpattiyaṃ. Yāvākiñcaññāyatanaṃ
  gantvāti evaṃ samathavipassanānaṃ yuganaddhabhāvāpādanavasena
  yāvākiñcaññāyatanaṃ tāva gantvā. Adhiṭṭheyyādikanti kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ
  ṭhapetvā visuṃ visuṃ ṭhitacīvarādiparikkhāragehādīnaṃ 1- aggiādinā
  avināsanādhiṭṭhānaṃ saṅghapaṭimānanasatthupakkosanānaṃ puretaraṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ
  sattāhabbhantare āyusaṅkhārappavattiolokananti catubbidhaṃ adhiṭṭhānādikaṃ
  pubbakiccaṃ katvā. Paṭipattirasassādanti jhānasukhaphalasukhādibhedaṃ
  samathavipassanāpaṭipattirasassādaṃ.
         Iti abhidhammatthavibhāviniyā nāma abhidhammatthasaṅgahavaṇṇanāya
                 navamaparicchedavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā.
                     --------------
                     Pakaraṇāvasānavaṇṇanā
      cārittena kulācārena sobhite visālakule udayo nibbatti
  yassa. Tena. Kammādivisayāya saddhāya abhivuddho parisuddho ca
  dānasīlādiguṇānaṃ udayo yassa. Tena. Nambavhayena nambanāmakena
  upāsakena parānukampaṃ sāsane sukhotaraṇaparipācanalakkhaṇaṃ parānuggahaṃ [2]-
  patthetvā yaṃ pakaraṇaṃ patthitaṃ abhiyācitaṃ taṃ ettāvatā pariniṭṭhitanti
  yojanā. Tena pakaraṇappasutena vipulena puññena paññāvadātena

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Sī. Ma. ṭhapita.... 2. Sī. Ma. paṇidhāyāti atthi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 278]
  Ariyamaggapaññāparisuddhena sīlādiguṇena sobhitā tatoyeva lajjino
  bhikkhū dhaññānaṃ adhivāsabhūtaṃ uditoditaṃ accantappasiddhaṃ tu mūlasomaṃ
  nāma vihāraṃ puññavibhavassa udayasaṅkhātāya maṅgalatthāya āyugantaṃ
  maññantu. Tattha nivāsino bhikkhū īdisā hontūti adhippāyo.
      Niṭṭhitāyaṃ abhidhammatthavibhāvinī nāma abhidhammatthasaṅgahaṭīkā.
                Ramme pulatthinagare nagarādhirāje
                raññā parakkamabhujena mahābhujena
                kārāpite vasati jetavane vihāre
                yo rammahammiyavarūpavanābhirāme.
                Sampannasīladamasaṃyamatositehi
                sammānito vasigaṇehi guṇākarehi
                patto munindavacanādisunekagaṇṭha- 1-
                jātesu cācariyataṃ mahitaṃ vidūhi.
                Ñāṇānubhāvamiha yassa ca sūcayantī
                saṃvaṇṇanā ca vinayaṭṭhakathādikānaṃ
                sāratthadīpanimukhā madhuratthasāraṃ
                sandīpanena sujanaṃ paritosayantī.
                Tassānukampamavalambiya sāriputta-
                therassa thāmagatasāraguṇākarassa
                yonekagaṇṭhavisayaṃ paṭutaṃ alatthaṃ
                tassesa ñāṇavibhavo vibhavekahetu.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Ma. munindavacanādisvanekagaṇṭha-.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 279]
          Sohametassa saṃsuddha- vāyāmassānubhāvato
          addhā sāsanadāyādo hessaṃ metteyyasatthuno.
          Jotayantaṃ tadā tassa sāsanaṃ suddhamānasaṃ
          passeyyaṃ sakkareyyañca garuṃ me sārisambhavaṃ.
          Dinehi catuvīsehi ṭīkāyaṃ niṭṭhitā yathā
          tathā kalyāṇasaṅkappā sīghaṃ ijjhantu pāṇinanti.
                Pakaraṇāvasānavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā 1-.
             Abhidhammatthavibhāvinī nāma abhidhammatthasaṅgahaṭīkā
